info: LidoSRv3/Audit/Verity/SszTxSimulation.lean:346:0: LidoSRv3.Audit.Verity.SszTxSimulation.ssz_tx_simulation_correct {State : Type} (input : TxInputs) (before after : State)
  (attempts : List CallAttempt) (trace : CommitTrace) (hwidths : exactTxWidths input) :
  exactTxWidths input ∧
    (digestPreimages input.toInputs).length = 7 ∧
      (runVerification input = VerificationOutcome.accept ↔ computedRoot input = input.expectedDepositDataRoot) ∧
        (runVerification input = VerificationOutcome.revertRootMismatch →
          (transactionObservation input before after attempts trace).committedState = before)
